# Beat Vegas Post-mortem: rated games vs outcomes

Generated by `scripts/post_mortem.py`. Pushes are excluded from the hit rate and count as staked. Every percentage carries its n and a 95% Wilson interval; P(>52.4%) is the factor ledger's Beta posterior against the -110 breakeven. Separating a 55% bettor from breakeven takes about 2,300 bets, so these tables rule out large edges and find large mistakes, nothing finer.

## Scope `hist_2023_25` (computed 2026-09-06T15:43:09Z, 3601 graded games)

- Ratings are the stored walk-forward gbm_v1 rows (2023-25); every line is a proxy, not a real 1H market.
- 'flat' grades at the 0.52 x full-game line the ratings were built on; 'step' grades at the fair spread-aware line (0.4975 / 0.5375 at spread 21+). The step column is the honest one.
- No Hard Rock prices existed historically, so the price, off-market and QB gates cannot be applied; 'followed the system' is the gap gate plus the weekly cap of 5.
- About 2,300 bets separate a 55% bettor from breakeven at 80% power; this data cannot confirm a realistic edge.

### What to change

- **CHANGE · score_gate** — under_score does not separate outcomes (48.9% at 60+ vs 49.0% below 47). Drop the 'score ≥ 53' clause from the confidence label; keep the score as a display chip.
- **OK · gap_gate** — Gap discriminates (55.3% at 1.75–3, 55.6% at 3+, vs 47.7% below 0); keep the 1.75 gate as a ranking rule. Followed-the-system hit rate against the fair line is 55.3% (n 166), at or above breakeven.
- **OK · gap_dip** — 1–1.75 band hits 50.0% vs 55.3% at 1.75–3 (difference +5.3 pts, about 0.9 SE). Within noise: 1.75 is a ranking convenience, not where an edge starts.
- **OK · strong_label** — 'Strong' (gap ≥ 3) hits 55.6% vs 55.3% for 1.75–3: no extra hit rate; keep 3.0 as a sort label only.
- **OK · division** — Non-FBS games hit 48.8% vs 48.9% FBS-vs-FBS (-0.2 pts). Little difference under the fair proxy.
- **WATCH · early_season** — Weeks 3–6 hit 46.7% vs 51.3% in weeks 7–10. Early-season gaps look weaker; consider discounting them or raising the games-played minimum.
- **OK · multiple_comparisons** — 176 comparisons were run; expect roughly 9 false positives at p < 0.05. Only q < 0.10 with |d| ≥ 0.2 is flagged.

### Does the gap survive spread and total controls?

- flat/fbs_only: standardized logistic coefficients (1934 games): gap_flat +0.13, spread_abs -0.06, full_game_total +0.10, wx_wind +0.00
- step/fbs_only: standardized logistic coefficients (1889 games): gap_step +0.08, spread_abs -0.02, full_game_total +0.10, wx_wind +0.01
- flat/all: standardized logistic coefficients (3601 games): gap_flat +0.05, spread_abs -0.15, full_game_total +0.04, wx_wind +0.01
- step/all: standardized logistic coefficients (3519 games): gap_step +0.04, spread_abs -0.10, full_game_total +0.04, wx_wind +0.03

### Overfitting control

- Deflated Sharpe of `cap5/step/fbs_only` against 24 tried configurations: 0.12 (≥ 0.95 would be significant).

## Scope `live_2026` (computed 2026-09-06T15:43:09Z, 68 graded games)

- The 2026 system has placed zero model bets by design (no model read in weeks 1-2); there is no 2026 record to grade.
- Every card item is a Hard Rock price read. 'hr' grades the under at Hard Rock's own number and price, 'market' at the consensus number when the card was built, 'market_close' at the consensus close.
- Counts, not rates, until a bucket has 30 graded games.
